## Executive Recommendation

`SimpleListPane` is a coherent, narrow derived control and should remain a standalone `ui/controls` class. The class owns the declarations and include envelope; the six source bodies remain on their exact by-memory children. The aggregate retains `[[CHILDREN]]`. Layout and vtable pages emit comments only, while their binary shape is regenerated by inheritance and virtual declarations. The compiler cluster stays no-owner for emission and source-blank.

Correct the `.rdata` boundary before treating coverage as exact: the primary complete-object-locator pointer at `0x00624c60` belongs with the SimpleListPane vtable family. Rename the vtable child and four touching predecessor/successor pages to use `0x00624c60`, then let the scoped validator recompute nesting. Do not merge ServerSelect code or resources into this unit.

The remaining research uncertainty is historical spelling/filing, not behavioral reconstruction. Ordinary documentation, range moves, and V01-V24 are complete; dated generated/manual snapshots prove the accepted payloads and routes at their recorded times. The only unchecked obligations are supervisor-owned then-current generated/manual reread with V25-V28 receipt closure and Gate 2B TG/I/P/D/R verification. No currently available evidence can prove original helper names or whether the original project once folded this tiny class into `ListPane.cpp`; that uncertainty caps confidence.

No investigable source-research question is deferred. Ordinary documentation defects are closed, and dated generated/manual snapshots prove the accepted payloads and routes at their recorded times; supervisor then-current reread plus V25-V28 receipt closure and the classified Gate 2B handoff remain unchecked. The raw-function intermediate-state gap is resolved by withdrawing those five chains, not by deferring discovery. Original helper spelling and exact original project file split remain evidence-bounded confidence limits.

## Evidence Standards Used

- Highest-weight evidence: current read-only IDA MCP decompilation/disassembly, exact byte ranges, direct xrefs, vtable stores, RTTI pointers, function extents, and PE-backed SHA256 slices.
- Corroboration: current ordinary by-file/by-class/by-memory/by-struct pages, accepted B001/B003 reports, source-tree documentation, and dated generated CPP/tracker/memory-coverage/manual-coverage snapshots.
- Negative evidence: raw-start xref and pointer searches, source filename/string searches, type catalog search, owned-global/resource/import searches, and neighboring-boundary inspection.
- Binary context: the code island is in executable text; padding is exact `0xcc`; vtable/RTTI data is compiler-generated `.rdata`. Storage role prevents raw RTTI/table emission even though it is file evidence.
- Tool limitation: IDA does not model five raw starts and lacks a complete `SimpleListPane` UDT. Exact disassembly and call semantics are sufficient for source behavior, while the missing model caps naming/type certainty.

## Evidence Checked

- IDA MCP: session/health, item boundaries, function lookup, decompile/disassembly, bytes, comments, types, xrefs, calls, vtable references, adjacent code/data, RTTI records, `RectBounds`, and absence of a `SimpleListPane` UDT.
- Physical binary: PE hash plus exact SHA256 slices for all six source bodies, three compiler bodies, seven gaps, seven vtable/COL/successor items, six RTTI graph records, both physical type-descriptor header heads, and the type-descriptor name storage.
- Documentation: target, class, constructor, aggregate, destructor, four helper children, compiler cluster, layout, vtable, four touching range pages, `ListPane` file/class, proposed source tree, the eight UID0002V2 support children and two UID00026F support children repaired in this callback, and accepted/stale historical reports.
- Generated/manual snapshot evidence: dated command-23511 source/header payloads, command-23531 tracker, command-23530 memory coverage, and all four manual coverage artifacts with eighteen exact single-match rows at B009's report-repair snapshot; these prove the stated payload/routes at that time but do not assert current shared identities.
- Whole-file negative searches: no owned source global/static/constant/string/table beyond compiler tables, no resource/import/third-party item, no raw-start callers or absolute pointer hits, no embedded `SimpleListPane.cpp`/`ListPane.cpp` filename, and no source membership after the hard ServerSelect boundary.
- Implementation callbacks: V01-V24 ran serially and the original final generated refresh completed. Additive C035 commands `000000023666-000000023675` each scanned exactly one repaired support page with exit `0`, `ok:1`, no warning/error, and generated refresh explicitly skipped. B009 intentionally skipped V25-V28, all IDA writes, all manual coverage text edits, lifecycle commands, report movement, and `execute_report`; the dated report-repair snapshot later found all eighteen manual rows already present at that time.

## Ranked Ownership Analysis

### 1. Standalone SimpleListPane control

- Evidence for: coherent class-specific code island; own RTTI and three vtable views; copied-text ownership policy; existing `ui/controls` source-tree placement; no feature resources.
- Evidence against: no embedded source filename and no direct ordinary callers.
- Decision: selected. It gives the narrowest complete source root and preserves all accepted routes.

### 2. Fold into ListPane

- Evidence for: thin subclass, inherited-only layout, direct base dependency.
- Evidence against: ListPane code is physically separate, SimpleListPane has a complete independent class identity, and no source-layout artifact proves a fold.
- Decision: rejected as a lower-confidence historical possibility; retain as a documented cap only.

### 3. ServerSelect/login placement

- Evidence for: immediate physical successor in code and `.rdata`.
- Evidence against: hard code/COL boundaries, reusable semantics, no login strings/resources/callers, and distinct RTTI.
- Decision: rejected.

### Proposed new file/grouping, if applicable

- Proposed owner/name/path: retain UID `0000NQ`, `NexusTK/ui/controls/SimpleListPane.cpp`, and generate sibling `SimpleListPane.h` from class UID `0000D8`.
- Likely full contents: class include envelope and child marker; six source methods; layout/vtable compiler comments; complete class declaration in H.
- Included related items: UIDs `0000D8`, `0001HP`, `000241`, `0002LE-0002LI`, `0001W3`, and corrected `0003D5`.
- Rejected related items: compiler page as source emitter, literal vtable/RTTI bytes, all padding, PointerSlotClearFreeHelper, and ServerSelect successor code/data.

};

Destructor UID `0002LE` formal CPP, unchanged:

```cpp
SimpleListPane::~SimpleListPane()
{
    for (int index = 0; index < GetItemCount(); ++index) {
        wchar_t **textSlot = static_cast<wchar_t **>(GetItem(index));
        if (*textSlot != NULL) {
            GetMemoryMan()->FreeBufferMemory(*textSlot);
            *textSlot = NULL;
        }
    }
}
```

Append UID `0002LF` formal CPP, unchanged:

```cpp
void SimpleListPane::AppendText(const wchar_t *text)
{
    const size_t textLength = wcslen(text);
    wchar_t *copiedText = static_cast<wchar_t *>(
        GetMemoryMan()->AllocateBufferMemory((textLength + 1) * sizeof(wchar_t)));
    wcscpy_s(copiedText, textLength + 1, text);
    InsertItem(GetItemCount(), &copiedText);
}
```

Insert UID `0002LG` formal CPP, unchanged:

```cpp
void SimpleListPane::InsertText(int index, const wchar_t *text)
{
    const size_t textLength = wcslen(text);
    wchar_t *copiedText = static_cast<wchar_t *>(
        GetMemoryMan()->AllocateBufferMemory((textLength + 1) * sizeof(wchar_t)));
    wcscpy_s(copiedText, textLength + 1, text);
    InsertItem(index, &copiedText);
}
```

Remove UID `0002LH` formal CPP, unchanged:

```cpp
void SimpleListPane::RemoveText(int index)
{
    wchar_t **textSlot = static_cast<wchar_t **>(GetItem(index));
    if (*textSlot != NULL) {
        GetMemoryMan()->FreeBufferMemory(*textSlot);
        *textSlot = NULL;
    }
    RemoveItems(index, 1);
}
```

Replace UID `0002LI` formal CPP, unchanged:

```cpp
void SimpleListPane::ReplaceText(int index, const wchar_t *text)
{
    wchar_t **textSlot = static_cast<wchar_t **>(GetItem(index));
    if (*textSlot != NULL) {
        GetMemoryMan()->FreeBufferMemory(*textSlot);
        *textSlot = NULL;
    }
    RemoveItems(index, 1);

    const size_t textLength = wcslen(text);
    wchar_t *copiedText = static_cast<wchar_t *>(
        GetMemoryMan()->AllocateBufferMemory((textLength + 1) * sizeof(wchar_t)));
    wcscpy_s(copiedText, textLength + 1, text);
    InsertItem(index, &copiedText);
}
```
